# Big-3 七接触固定基路径证书

本证书是《Big-3 两条 a 切口修复》§57 的有限数学数据。固定 n=10、所选标签 p,q,x,y,z,w=1,…,6，标记基为 (7,8,9,10)。42 条记录由 20 个完整 V2 原生路径叶、11 个高层展开记录及 11 个单切口完整子圈引用组成。20 个叶逐一穷尽 1440 个原生顶点，总计检查 28800 个叶顶点；不枚举完整 S10。

检查程序仅使用 Python 标准库，独立重建实际 V2 原生因子、切口和子域分割，并检查逐生成元路径、完整子引用、所有旧开关切口、字面 c 连接、度数及全部边和顶点的路径穷尽。高层单切口记录明确依赖主文已证明的完整 V3、V4、V5、V6 单圈及基标签末项 b 保留定理；程序没有独立证明这些普遍定理。§57 的全参数结论另依赖 §56 的实际标记完整路径细化，不能由有限计算推出。本文不声称 Lean kernel 或官方验收。

原始证书 SHA256：`3b48dd9f533ee6e24e189d51e0ae1e979c161b2ee21e27b4a42090211c12495d`。检查程序 SHA256：`30ee323973c6a9171833f25f7feda00a04f849710123e926a24ad0a5ffcd6a09`。以下两个代码块完整保留相应字节。将 json 块另存为 `v7_pq_fixed_base_path_certificate.json`，将 python 块另存为同目录的 `verify_v7_standalone.py`，执行 `python3 -B verify_v7_standalone.py`。正常结果为 `FIXED_V7_PQ_CERTIFICATE_VERIFIED 42 V2_native_vertices 28800`。

## 独立检查程序

```python
"""Self-contained verifier for the frozen n10 V7 pq native path certificate.

Only Python standard library imports. Reconstructs the actual raw V2 factor,
checks all raw paths and every higher old cut, literal c, child reference,
complete child-domain partition, boundary degree and exhaustive trace.

Higher one-cut references invoke the previously proved complete V3..V6 cycle
and base-last-b-retention theorems. This program proves the precise finite
identity conditional on those mathematical theorems; it is not the uniform
refinement proof, a Lean kernel proof or an official Hamilton-cycle judge.

Run with Python assertions enabled (do not pass -O):
    python3 -B verify_v7_standalone.py
Place the unchanged v7_pq_fixed_base_path_certificate.json next to this file.
"""
import json
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from pathlib import Path


def rotations(w):
    w = tuple(w)
    return [w[i:] + w[:i] for i in range(len(w))]


def bracelet(w):
    return min(rotations(w) + rotations(tuple(w)[::-1]))


def inserted(w, label):
    w = tuple(w)
    return {bracelet(w[:i] + (label,) + w[i:]) for i in range(len(w))}


def step(v, letter):
    count = {'a': 10, 'b': 9, 'c': 8}[letter]
    return v[:count][::-1] + v[count:]


def walk(v, word):
    result = [tuple(v)]
    for letter in word:
        result.append(step(result[-1], letter))
    return result


def leaf_factor():
    base = tuple(range(3, 11))
    circles = set().union(*(inserted(c, 1) for c in inserted(base, 2)))
    assert len(circles) == 9 * 8
    vertices = set().union(*(set(rotations(c) + rotations(c[::-1])) for c in circles))
    assert len(vertices) == 1440
    graph = {v: {step(v, 'a'), step(v, 'b')} for v in vertices}
    switches = [walk((1,) + base + (2,), ('ac' + 'bc' * 7) * 2)]
    switches += [walk(base[:i] + (2,) + base[i:] + (1,), 'bc' * 9)
                 for i in range(1, len(base))]
    selected = set()
    for switch in switches:
        assert switch[0] == switch[-1]
        for i, (u, v) in enumerate(zip(switch, switch[1:])):
            assert u in graph and v in graph
            key = tuple(sorted((u, v)))
            assert key not in selected
            selected.add(key)
            assert (v in graph[u]) == (i % 2 == 0)
            if i % 2 == 0:
                graph[u].remove(v)
                graph[v].remove(u)
            else:
                graph[u].add(v)
                graph[v].add(u)
    assert all(len(neighbors) == 2 for neighbors in graph.values())
    reached = {next(iter(graph))}
    pending = list(reached)
    while pending:
        for vertex in graph[pending.pop()]:
            if vertex not in reached:
                reached.add(vertex)
                pending.append(vertex)
    assert reached == set(graph)
    return graph




def act(vertex, word, n):
    vertex = tuple(vertex)
    for letter in word:
        count = {'a': n, 'b': n - 1, 'c': n - 2}[letter]
        vertex = vertex[:count][::-1] + vertex[count:]
    return vertex


def T(vertex, n):
    vertices = [tuple(vertex)]
    for letter in ('ac' + 'bc' * (n - 3)) * 2:
        vertices.append(act(vertices[-1], letter, n))
    return vertices


def oriented_insertion(C, A, x):
    C, A = tuple(C), tuple(A)
    offset = C.index(x)
    directed = C[offset:] + C[:offset]
    if directed[1:] not in rotations(A):
        directed = (directed[0],) + directed[:0:-1]
    assert directed[1:] in rotations(A)
    return directed


def make_block(base, n):
    assert n == 10 and tuple(base) == tuple(range(3, 11))
    return leaf_factor(), tuple(base)


def check_fixed_domains(data):
    assert data['n'] == 10
    assert data['selected_prefix'] == list(range(1, 7))
    assert data['marked_base'] == [7, 8, 9, 10]
    prefix = (1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 8, 9, 10)
    root_r = [prefix[i:] + prefix[:i] + (7,) for i in range(9)]
    root_l = [act(vertex, 'b', 10) for vertex in root_r]
    assert data['outer_cuts'] == [[list(root_r[i]), list(root_l[i])] for i in range(7)]
    assert len(set(root_r[:7] + root_l[:7])) == 14
    assert all(act(root_l[i], 'c', 10) == root_r[(i + 1) % 9] for i in range(9))
    assert data['hidden_loops'] == 0
    assert Counter(row['k'] for row in data['records']) == {2: 20, 3: 9, 4: 8, 5: 4, 6: 1}
    assert data['actual_V2_queries'] == data['raw_V2_certificate_rows'] == 20
    assert data['all_parameter_proof'] is False and data['full_S_n_enumeration'] is False
    for row in data['records']:
        k, base = row['k'], tuple(row['base'])
        assert base == tuple(range(k + 1, 11))
        for cut in row['cuts']:
            for vertex in cut:
                assert sorted(vertex) == list(range(1, 11))
                assert bracelet(tuple(a for a in vertex if a > k)) == bracelet(base)
        if k > 2 and len(row['cuts']) > 1:
            expected = {(k - 1, (k,) + word) for word in rotations(base)}
            if k == 4:
                special = (4,) + base
                expected.remove((3, special))
                expected.update((2, (3,) + word) for word in rotations(special))
            actual = {(child['k'], tuple(child['base'])) for child in row['children']}
            assert len(actual) == len(row['children']) and actual == expected
            for child in row['children']:
                ck, cb = child['k'], tuple(child['base'])
                assert ck < k
                for cut in child['cuts']:
                    for vertex in cut:
                        assert bracelet(tuple(a for a in vertex if a > ck)) == bracelet(cb)
    assert len({bracelet(tuple(a for a in vertex if a > 6)) for vertex in root_r[:7]}) == 1
    assert len({bracelet(tuple(a for a in vertex if a > 6)) for vertex in root_r}) == 3
    assert act(root_l[-1], 'c', 10) == root_r[0]


def edge(u, v):
    return tuple(sorted((tuple(u), tuple(v))))


def cuts_key(cuts):
    return tuple(sorted(edge(u, v) for u, v in cuts))


def matching_map(pairs):
    result = {}
    for u, v in pairs:
        u, v = tuple(u), tuple(v) if v is not None else None
        assert u not in result
        result[u] = v
        if v is not None:
            assert v not in result and v != u
            result[v] = u
    return result


def locate(k, base, vertex):
    child_base = oriented_insertion(tuple(a for a in vertex if a >= k), base, k)
    if k == 4 and child_base == (4,) + tuple(base):
        lower = tuple(a for a in vertex if a >= 3)
        return 2, oriented_insertion(lower, child_base, 3)
    return k - 1, child_base


def verify():
    data = json.loads(Path(__file__).with_name('v7_pq_fixed_base_path_certificate.json').read_text())
    check_fixed_domains(data)
    n = data['n']
    records = data['records']
    index = {(r['k'], tuple(r['base']), cuts_key(r['cuts'])): r for r in records}
    assert len(index) == len(records)
    leaf_vertices = 0
    for row in records:
        k, base = row['k'], tuple(row['base'])
        cuts = cuts_key(row['cuts'])
        expected = matching_map(row['matching'])
        assert row['hidden_loops'] == 0
        if k > 2 and len(cuts) == 1:
            u, v = cuts[0]
            # This is the proved base-last-b-retention interface of the child.
            assert u[-1] in base and v[-1] in base and act(u, 'b', n) == v
            assert expected == {u: v, v: u}
            continue
        if k == 2:
            factor, _ = make_block(base, n)
            for u, v in cuts:
                assert v in factor[u]
                factor[u].remove(v)
                factor[v].remove(u)
            seen, actual = set(), {}
            for path in row['raw_V2_paths']:
                start, end = tuple(path['start']), tuple(path['end'])
                current, local = start, {start}
                assert len(factor[start]) in (0, 1)
                for letter in path['word']:
                    nxt = act(current, letter, n)
                    assert nxt in factor[current] and nxt not in local
                    local.add(nxt)
                    current = nxt
                assert current == end and len(factor[end]) in (0, 1)
                assert path['edges'] == len(path['word'])
                assert seen.isdisjoint(local)
                seen.update(local)
                if path.get('isolated'):
                    assert start == end and not factor[start] and not path['word']
                    actual[start] = None
                else:
                    assert start != end
                    actual[start], actual[end] = end, start
            assert seen == set(factor) and actual == expected
            assert len(seen) == row['raw_V2_vertices_exhausted']
            leaf_vertices += len(seen)
            continue
        if k == 4:
            switch = T((3,) + base + (1, 2, 4), n)
            old = list(zip(switch[0:-1:2], switch[1:-1:2]))
            connectors = list(zip(switch[1:-1:2], switch[2::2]))
        else:
            prefix = ((1, 2) if k == 3 else (2, 1) + tuple(range(3, k))) + base
            ports = [prefix[i:] + prefix[:i] + (k,) for i in range(n - 1)]
            old = [(u, act(u, 'b', n)) for u in ports]
            connectors = [(v, ports[(i + 1) % (n - 1)]) for i, (_, v) in enumerate(old)]
        assert cuts_key(old) == cuts_key(row['actual_old_switch_edges'])
        assert cuts_key(connectors) == cuts_key(row['actual_c_connectors'])
        groups = defaultdict(list)
        for u, v in old + list(cuts):
            child = locate(k, base, u)
            assert child == locate(k, base, v)
            groups[child].append((u, v))
        assert set(groups) == {(ch['k'], tuple(ch['base'])) for ch in row['children']}
        edges = Counter()
        isolated = set()
        for child in row['children']:
            ck, cb = child['k'], tuple(child['base'])
            assert cuts_key(groups[(ck, cb)]) == cuts_key(child['cuts'])
            relabel = {int(u): v for u, v in child['forward_relabeling'].items()}
            assert relabel == dict(list(zip(range(1, ck + 1), range(1, ck + 1)))
                                  + list(zip(cb, range(ck + 1, n + 1))))
            ccuts = [(tuple(relabel[a] for a in u), tuple(relabel[a] for a in v))
                     for u, v in child['cuts']]
            cbase = tuple(range(ck + 1, n + 1))
            assert tuple(child['canonical_base']) == cbase
            assert cuts_key(ccuts) == cuts_key(child['canonical_cuts'])
            target = index[(ck, cbase, cuts_key(ccuts))]
            cm = matching_map(child['matching'])
            canonical_cm = {tuple(relabel[a] for a in u):
                            tuple(relabel[a] for a in v) if v is not None else None
                            for u, v in cm.items()}
            assert canonical_cm == matching_map(target['matching'])
            for u, v in cm.items():
                if v is None:
                    isolated.add(u)
                elif u < v:
                    edges[('child full path', edge(u, v))] += 1
        for u, v in connectors:
            assert act(u, 'c', n) == v
            edges[('literal c', edge(u, v))] += 1
        degrees = Counter()
        for (_, (u, v)), multiplicity in edges.items():
            degrees[u] += multiplicity
            degrees[v] += multiplicity
        incidence = Counter(v for cut in cuts for v in cut)
        all_vertices = set(degrees) | isolated
        assert all(degrees[v] == 2 - incidence[v] for v in all_vertices)
        assert set(expected) == set(incidence)
        used, actual = Counter(), {}
        used_vertices = set()
        for path in row['exhaustive_boundary_traces']:
            start, end = tuple(path['start']), tuple(path['end'])
            if path.get('isolated'):
                assert start == end and start in isolated and not path['steps']
                assert degrees[start] == 0 and start not in used_vertices
                used_vertices.add(start)
                actual[start] = None
                continue
            current, local = start, {start}
            for u, label, v in path['steps']:
                u, v = tuple(u), tuple(v)
                assert u == current and v not in local
                used[(label, edge(u, v))] += 1
                local.add(v)
                current = v
            assert current == end and start != end
            assert degrees[start] == degrees[end] == 1
            assert used_vertices.isdisjoint(local)
            used_vertices.update(local)
            actual[start], actual[end] = end, start
        assert used == edges and actual == expected and used_vertices == all_vertices
    root = index[(6, tuple(data['marked_base']), cuts_key(data['outer_cuts']))]
    ports = {tuple(v): name for i, (u, v) in enumerate(data['outer_cuts'])
             for v, name in ((u, 'R' + str(i)), (v, 'L' + str(i)))}
    pairs = [[ports[tuple(u)], ports[tuple(v)]] for u, v in root['matching']]
    assert pairs == data['beta_pairs']
    assert {frozenset(pair) for pair in pairs} == {
        frozenset(pair) for pair in [('R0', 'R1'), ('L1', 'L5'), ('L2', 'L3'),
                                    ('R2', 'L0'), ('L4', 'R3'), ('R4', 'L6'),
                                    ('R5', 'R6')]}
    print('FIXED_V7_PQ_CERTIFICATE_VERIFIED', len(records),
          'V2_native_vertices', leaf_vertices, flush=True)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    verify()
```
